## Document 013: 1802台玻 群益

Source PDF: all report pdf/1802台玻 群益.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-11-04
Pages: 3
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

產業速報  
                                        2026 年 11 月 3 日

1 群益投顧 
台玻 (1802 TT ) 3Q26 營收 141.34 億 元 ，QoQ+17.17% 、YoY+31.76% ； 營 業 毛 利 44.67
億 元 ，QoQ+58.74% 、 YoY+257.07% ， 毛 利 率 約 31.60% ， 主 因受惠 於 Low Dk2 、 Low 
CTE 等 高毛利 T-glass 產品 出 貨 比 重 拉 升 ， 且 毛 利 率 較2Q26 提升 8.27% 至 31.60% ，
亦 優 於 原 預 期 之 26.23% ；稅後淨利 27.05 億元， QoQ+113.66% 、 YoY+876.53% ，稅
後 EPS 0.93 元， 均 優於預期 。 鑒於 Low Dk2/Low CTE 出 貨 動 能 持 續 攀 升， 加 上高階
玻纖布 訂 單 能 見 度 已 達 2027 年，且 2028 年 訂 單 洽 談 順 暢。研究部 預估台玻 2027/2028
年稅後 EPS 分別為 4.37 /6.15 元。給予投資建議 Buy ， 目 標 價 81 元。

研 究 員 ： 蔡 季 衡   
A52320@cim.capital.com.tw

台玻 3Q26 毛 利 率 攀 至 31.60% ， 優 於 預 期 ：  
台玻 3Q26 營收 141.34 億元， QoQ+17.17% 、 YoY+31.76% ；營業毛利 44.67
億 元 ，QoQ+58.74% 、 YoY+257.07% ， 毛 利 率 約 31.60% ； 營 業 利 益 33.84 億
元， QoQ+121.90% 、YoY+2,186.48% ；稅 後 淨 利 27.05 億 元，QoQ+113.66% 、
YoY+876.53% ， 稅 後 EPS 0.93 元。  
研 究 部 評 析 ：  
台玻 3Q26 受惠 於 Low Dk2 、Low CTE 等 高 毛 利 T-glass 產品 出 貨 比 重 拉 升，
Low Dk1 占比 持 穩 ， 帶 動 3Q26 營 業 毛 利 QoQ+58.74% 、 YoY+257.07% ，且
毛利率較 2Q26 提升 8.27% 至 31.60% ， 亦 優 於 原 預 期 之 26.23% 。 因 此 3Q26
稅後淨利 27.05 億 元 、 稅 後 EPS 0.93 元， 均 優 於 原 預 期 之 20.47 億 元 、0.70
元。 1~3Q26 台 玻 累 計 營 收 達 364.40 億 元 ， YoY+18.69% ， 毛 利 率 25.38% ，
稅後淨利 45.65 億 元 ， YoY 由 虧 轉 盈 ， 稅 後 EPS 1.57 元。  
台玻 為 因應 供 應 鏈 對 於高 階 玻 纖 布之 強 勁 需 求，擬 增 建 及 冷 修 窯 爐，以 擴 充
產 能 ，並 提 升 市 占 率。11/02/2026 董 事 會 通 過 ，桃 園 廠 、 鹿 港 廠 窯 爐 增 建 及
子 公 司 台 嘉 蚌 埠 玻 璃 纖 維 有 限 公 司 冷 修擴 充 案 ， 預 估 投 入 金 額 約新台幣 16.3
億 元 及 人 民 幣 11.5 億 元 。  
展望 2027 ~2028 年， Low CTE 當前 出 貨 量 已 較 2026 年 初 顯 著 增 加 ， 預 期 營
收 佔 比 將 於 2027 年 陸 續 提 升 ， 並 於 2028 年 伴 隨 近 年 陸 續 擴 建 之 新 產 能 開
出 後 ，追趕 Low Dk2 。 研 究 部預期 2027 年 Low Dk2 營 收 比 重 仍 將 高 於 Low 
Dk1 及 Low CTE 。目 前 台 玻 高 階 玻 纖 布訂 單 能 見 度已達 2027 年，且 2028 年
訂 單 洽 談 順 暢 。 若 供 應 鏈 產 品 需 求無大 規 模 質 變 ， 台玻 2028 年 玻 纖 布 事 業
群 仍 將 持 續 成 長 。研 究 部 預 估 台 玻 4Q26 底 ~2027 年 玻 纖 布 營 收 比 重 有 望 超
越 平 板 玻 璃 和 玻 璃 器 皿 。 預 估 2027 /2028 年稅後 EPS 分別為 4.37 /6.15 元 。  
投 資 建 議 ：  
台玻 3Q26 營收 141.34 億元， QoQ+17.17% 、 YoY+31.76% ；營業毛利 44.67
億 元，QoQ+58.74% 、YoY+257.07% ，毛 利 率 約 31.60% ，主 因受惠 於 Low Dk2 、
Low CTE 等 高 毛 利 T-glass 產品 出 貨 比 重 拉 升 ， 且 毛 利 率 較2Q26 提升 8.27%
至 31.60% ，亦 優 於 原 預 期 之 26.23% ；稅 後 淨 利 27.05 億 元，QoQ+113.66% 、
YoY+876.53% ，稅後 EPS 0.93 元， 均 優 於 預 期 。 鑒 於 Low Dk2/Low CTE 出
貨 動 能 持 續 攀 升，加 上高 階 玻 纖 布 訂 單 能 見 度 已 達 2027 年，且 2028 年訂單
洽 談 順 暢 。研 究 部 預 估 台 玻 2027/2028 年 稅 後 EPS 分別為 4.37 /6.15 元。給
予 投 資 建 議 Buy ， 目 標 價 81 元。

### Page 3

產業速報  
                                        2026 年 11 月 3 日

3 群益投顧 
  【 投 資 評 等 說 明 】 
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15%≤ 首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間<35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy)  5%≤ 首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間<15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無 法 由 基 本 面 給 予 投 資 評 等 
預 期 近 期 股 價 將 處 於 盤 整 
建 議 降 低 持 股

【 投 資 評 等 調 整 機 制 】 
  投 資 評 等 係「 首 次 」給 予 特 定 個 股 投 資 評 等 時，其 前 一 日 收 盤 價 相 較3 個月
目 標 價 之 潛 在 上 漲 空 間 計 算 而 得 。 個 股 投 資 評 等 分 為四 個 等 級 ， 定 義 如 上 。
爾 後 的 投 資 評 等係 依 循 「 首 次 評 等 」 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。  
「 強 力 買 進 」、「 買 進 」及 「 區 間 操 作 」均有 upside 目 標 價 。差 別 僅 在 於 ，
首 次 評 等 時 潛 在 報 酬 率 不 同 。 「 中 立 」 則 無 目 標 價 。 
一 旦 我 們 給 予 特 定 個 股「 強 力 買 進 」、「 買 進 」或「 區 間 操 作 」之 投 資 評 等，
就 是 責 任 的 開 始，爾 後 將 透 過 各 式 研 究 報 告 作 定 期 性、持 續 性 追 蹤 基 本 面 及
股 價 變 化 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。 
停 止 推 薦 情 境 ： 
1.  達 目 標 價 。 
2.  雖 未 達 原 訂 目 標 價 ， 但 檢 視 基 本 面 、 訊 息 面 、 籌 碼 面 等 多 方 訊 息 ， 研
判 股 價 上 漲 空 間 已 然 有 限 ， 將 適 時 出 具 降 評 報 告 。 
3.  推 薦 後 股 價 不 漲 反 跌 。

【免責聲明】  
  本 研 究 報 告 僅 提 供 予 特 定 人 之 客 戶 作 為 參 考 資 料「 非 經 同 意 不 得 轉 載 」 。我
們 並 不 確 保 此 資 訊 的 完 整 性 與 正 確 性，投 資 人 應 了 解，報 告 中 有 關 未 來 預 測
之 陳 述 可 能 不 會 實 現，因 而 不 應 被 依 賴。而 且 此 報 告 並 非 根 據 特 定 投 資 目 的
或 依 預 定 對 象 之 財 務 狀 況 所 撰 寫 出 來 的，因 此，此 研 究 報 告 的 目 的，既 非 對
投 資 人 於 買 賣 證 券、選 擇 權、期 貨 或 其 他 證 券 相 關 之 衍 生 性 商 品 提 供 詢 價 服
務，亦 非 作 為 進 行 交 易 的 要 約。投 資 人 應 注 意 到 相 關 證 券 之 價 值 及 收 益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

足許の経済・金融状況と注目ポイント
1
（注）予想はLSEG、ブルームバーグが取りまとめた予想コンセンサス
（出所）LSEG、ブルームバーグより、みずほ総合研究所調査部作成
2026. 10.27
先週（10/19～10/26）発表の主な経済指標
米生産活動は堅調。AI関連投資を背景にコンピューター・電子
製品や機械などがけん引。一方、データセンター建設に伴う需要
増加が、他産業の生産や建設活動を圧迫する兆候も
コア消費者物価は再び2％超え。特殊要因を除くサービス価格
も加速し、人件費転嫁の兆し。連合は2027年春闘で6％中心
を要求。中小企業の余力に課題も、物価上振れに注意
注目点② サービス物価の加速で重み増す2027年春闘戦線注目点①AI投資の恩恵拡大、副作用もじわり顕在化
総合PMIは57.6と高水準を維持。新規失業保険申
請件数も19.4万件と低水準で解雇の動きは引き続
き限定的。一方で、住宅着工件数や住宅建設業
者の景況感は悪化、高金利の影響が継続

日本：輸出の好調が継続。2026年度の企業業績はAI関連の製造業を中心に大幅増益へ
12
 7～9月期の輸出金額は前期比＋3.3％と、旺盛なAI需要を背景に増加が継続
― 電子部品・半導体製造装置などに加え、非鉄金属・電気計測機器・原動機・重電機器などのAIインフラ関連財がけん引

 輸出金額は当面、前年比で増加が続く見通し。2026年度の企業業績はAI関連の製造業を中心に大幅増益へ
― 石化製品の価格上昇はコスト増要因となる一方、燃料油の政府補助金が原油高による収益下押し圧力を幾分緩和

14

― 国内物価高や円安などが支えとなり消費単価は同＋6.1％と増加。一方、訪日外客数は中東情勢悪化による減便や航

―2026年冬の国際線旅客便数の回復ペースは緩慢。中国路線の回復は見込みにくく、目先の訪日外客数は伸び悩みへ

Source PDF: all report pdf/20261005_投資週報_製造業加速擴張，AI需求推動市場.pdf
Report source: Yuanta (投資週報)
Date: 2026-10-05
Pages: 1
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

金融市場投資週報                 2026 年 10 月 05 日
上週重點訊息
◼ 美國製造業維持擴張，成本壓力升高：9 月 ISM 製造業調查顯示，美國製造業連續第九個月
位於擴張區間，新訂單與就業分項均見改善，反映接單與用人需求仍有支撐。然而，價格指數
由 8 月的 71.1 升至 9 月的 77.9，能源、金屬及關稅相關成本上升，加上供應商交貨偏慢，企
業面臨更大成本壓力 。 後續須觀察企業能否轉嫁成本 ， 以及通膨黏性對聯準會政策判斷的影響 。
◼ 日本短觀顯示製造業改善、服務業略緩：日本銀行 10 月 1 日公布的 9 月短觀顯示，大型製造

業景氣判斷指數由 6 月的＋22 升至＋24，大型非製造業則由＋37 降至＋35。企業預估 12 月
兩項指數分別回落至＋21 與＋30，顯示現況仍有支撐，後續看法則偏審慎。製造業改善有助
支撐日股獲利預期，但服務業放緩及日銀政策路徑仍須持續觀察。
◼ 台灣製造業活動加速擴張，AI 需求續強：10 月 1 日公布的製造業調查顯示，台灣 9 月製造業
活動擴張速度創 2021 年 8 月以來最快，AI 相關應用需求仍是重要推力。國發會 9 月 29 日公
布的 8 月景氣對策信號續呈紅燈，綜合判斷分數為 41 分，與 7 月持平，領先及同時指標皆續
升。科技供應鏈景氣仍有支撐，但成長動能能否擴及其他產業，仍是觀察後續景氣的重點。
市場展望
◼ AI 需求持續擴張，關注財報季表現： AI 基礎建設需求未受利率環境影響，而是持續擴張，帶
動光通訊、CCL、ABF 載板 、記憶體、先進製程與封裝等關鍵供應鏈維持強勁成長，市場表
現由全面上漲轉向結構性分化。隨新一代 AI 伺服器放量、CSP 資本支出維持高檔，具供給優

勢、定價能力及訂單能見度延伸至 2027 年的龍頭廠商仍可望受惠。10 月中旬起陸續公布的
第三季財報，若結果持續表現亮眼，可望為市場再添動能，屆時具 AI 供應鏈題材的台股與日
股亦可望受惠。
◼ 債券佈局聚焦 5~10 年期美國公債及投等債：雖然貨幣政策風險 下降有助於穩定長期通膨預
期，但政府與企業發債供給增加所帶來的排擠效應，以及美國經濟成長展現韌性，仍可能使長
天期利率面臨上行壓力。因此，在當前利率環境下，5~10 年期債券較能兼顧票息收益與存續
期間風險管理。投資級債方面，彭博美國投資級公司債指數利差約 80-90 個基點，雖然利差
偏窄，但高基準利率環境仍提供具吸引力的絕對收益水準，因此現階段5~10 年期美國公債及
投資級債的風險報酬結構相對較佳。

### Page 2

本報告係根據研究資料及各項可靠數據作成，兆豐銀行保留報告內容所有權利，未經本行許可，請勿引

1

國際經濟金融週報內容摘要 
美 
國 
美國經濟維持穩健擴張，通膨壓力略升溫，Fed 維持利率不變，期中選舉進入
最後衝刺階段 
經濟 
⚫ 美國第三季實質 GDP 季增年率(初值)由上季的 2.2%升至 3.4%，成長主
要受民間消費 、 企業投資及庫存回補帶動 。 其中個人消費貢獻2.52 個百
分點、私人投資貢獻 3.27 個百分點，惟淨出口拖累 3.32 個百分點，整
體經濟仍維持穩健擴張。 
⚫ 9 月美國新屋銷售年化 67.6 萬戶，月減 0.6%、年減 5.3%，主要是中西
部及南部地區銷售下滑拖累整體表現。房價雖較上月回升，但仍低於去
年同期；供給月數 8.4 個月，顯示市場仍呈供給充裕、需求偏弱格局。 
⚫ 美國 9 月個人消費支出(PCE)物價指數年增率由 3.4%升至 3.6%，核心
PCE 物價指數年增率亦由 3.0%升至 3.1%，通膨再度升溫。儘管實質所
得受物價上漲影響而下滑，消費支出仍持續成長，反映家庭需求及內需
動能仍具韌性。 
⚫ 10 月 FOMC 會議以 10 比 2 決議將聯邦基金利率目標區間維持於 3.75%
至 4.0%。決策聲明指出，美國經濟活動表現強勁，就業市場變化不大，
通膨持續高於 2%目標，將依後續數據評估適當政策立場，以確保通膨
回歸目標。兩名委員主張升息 1 碼，顯示內部對通膨風險仍存分歧。主
席 Kevin Warsh 強調將防止能源價格上漲擴散至整體物價 ， 並宣布2027
年起部分 FOMC 會議改為策略討論會議，以強化長期政策研議。 
重要 
事件 
⚫ 10 月 26 日路透社民調顯示，川普施政支持率 32%，略高於前週；國會
選舉政黨支持度則由民主黨 47%領先共和黨 36%。截至 10 月 31 日，
提前及郵寄投票已逾 5,100 萬人。整體而言，期中選舉結果將影響未來
國會版圖、施政推動及貿易政策方向，備受市場關注。 
短評 
⚫ 美國第三季 GDP 成長優於前季，主要受民間消費、企業投資及庫存回
補帶動，顯示內需與企業活動仍具韌性；惟新屋銷售持續疲弱、房市需
求未明顯改善，反映高利率環境仍抑制部分經濟活動。另一方面，PCE
通膨再度升溫，核心通膨降幅有限，顯示物價壓力仍存。在經濟穩健且
通膨高於目標情況下，Fed 維持利率不變並釋出偏鷹訊號，後續貨幣政
策料將維持審慎，持續關注通膨與就業數據變化。

### Page 3

本報告係根據研究資料及各項可靠數據作成，兆豐銀行保留報告內容所有權利，未經本行許可，請勿引
用相關內容。

2

歐 
元 
區 
歐洲央行如期暫停升息，12 月升息機率較高 
經濟 ⚫ 歐元區第三季 GDP 季增率為 0.4%。 
重要 
事件 ⚫ 歐洲央行在 10 月 29 日宣布維持政策利率不變。 
短評 
⚫ 第三季受到物價壓力上升、地緣政治緊張局勢等因素影響，對消費及投
資需求構成負面因素，但世足賽以及德國財政支出擴張的支持下，仍使
歐元區經濟保持正向的成長表現。 
⚫ 歐洲央行仍認為近期高漲的能源價格對通膨的壓力相當大，因此，除非
經濟成長受到超出預期的衝擊、能源價格下跌或金融環境急劇收緊，一
般預期歐洲央行在 12 月的政策會議將宣布升息一碼。 
日 
本 
日本央行暫停升息，通膨壓力續存，升息預期升溫 
經濟 
⚫ 9 月工業生產月增 2.2%。 
⚫ 9 月零售月減 0.7%。 
⚫ 10 月東京核心 CPI 年增 2.6%。 
重要 
事件 ⚫ 日本央行維持利率於 1.25%不變，2027 年起加快 ETF 出售速度。 
短評 
⚫ 通膨仍具黏性，在通膨已大致達到 2%的目標、金融環境仍偏寬鬆的情
況下，日本央行貨幣政策正常化方向應不致改變。儘管本次暫緩升息，
但隨通膨風險持續上行，後續仍具升息空間，貨幣政策可望維持漸進緊
縮基調。

一、 美國—經濟維持穩健擴張，通膨壓力略升溫，Fed 維持利率不變，期中選
舉進入最後衝刺階段 
2026 年第三季美國實質國內生產毛額(GDP)季增年率初值為 3.4%， 高於第二
季之 2.2%，整體景氣持續穩健擴張。就成長貢獻而言，個人消費支出仍為經濟成
長主要支柱，貢獻 2.52 個百分點，其中服務消費及商品消費分別貢獻 1.89 個百
分點及 0.63 個百分點 ， 反映民間需求維持穩健 ； 私人投資則貢獻3.27 個百分點 ，
為本季成長最大亮點，其中固定投資貢獻1.66 個百分點，而企業庫存回補亦額外
貢獻 1.61 個百分點。對外貿易方面，淨出口拖累經濟成長 3.32 個百分點，其中
出口減少造成 0.68 個百分點負面影響；進口增加則拖累 2.64 個百分點。政府消
費支出與投資則受聯邦政府非國防支出增加帶動，貢獻 0.90 個百分點。此外，第
三季個人消費支出(PCE)物價指數及核心 PCE 物價指數分別年增 2.1%及 2.5%，
均低於第二季水準。整體而言，美國第三季經濟成長主要受民間消費、企業投資
及庫存回補支撐 ， 雖貿易逆差擴大對GDP 形成明顯拖累 ， 但內需表現仍具韌性 ，
顯示美國經濟基本面維持穩健。 
2026 年 9 月美國新屋銷售經季節性調整後年化戶數為 67.6 萬戶，較上月減
少 0.6%， 並較去年同期下降5.3%， 顯示在高利率環境下住宅需求仍較去年疲弱 。
地區別表現分歧，其中東北部及西部分別較上月增加 28.0%及 21.4%，惟中西部
及南部分別減少 7.3%及 6.6%，尤其占全美銷售比重最大的南部地區回落，成為
拖累整體銷售的主因。價格方面，9 月新屋銷售中位價為 40.04 萬美元，較上月
上升 3.9%，但較去年同期下跌 3.9%，儘管房價高於上月，惟仍面臨修正壓力。
供給方面，9 月底待售新屋數量為 47.6 萬戶，較上月減少1.0%，較去年同期減少
2.3%；按目前銷售速度計算，供給月數為 8.4 個月，低於上月的 8.5 個月，但高
於去年同期的 8.2 個月。整體而言，9 月美國新屋銷售較前月小幅下滑且年減幅
度擴大，反映購屋需求仍未明顯改善；雖然部分地區銷售回升且庫存略有下降，
但供給月數仍維持相對偏高水準，顯示新屋市場仍處於供給較充裕、需求偏弱的
格局，後續仍須觀察利率走勢及就業市場變化對住宅需求的影響。 
2026 年 9 月美國個人消費支出(PCE)物價指數月增 0.5%， 高於8 月之 0.3%，

年增率則由 3.4%升至 3.6%； 扣除食品及能源後之核心PCE 月增率維持於 0.3%，
年增率由 3.0%微升至 3.1%，能源價格回升及服務價格上漲持續對整體物價形成
支撐，通膨再度升溫。另一方面，個人所得月增 0.2%，可支配所得(DPI)亦月增
0.2%，個人消費支出則月增 0.5%，增幅高於所得成長，顯示家庭消費需求仍具韌
性；惟受物價上漲影響，實質可支配所得月減0.3%，實質個人消費支出則持平，
反映名目支出增加部分已被物價上升所抵銷。整體而言，美國 9 月消費活動維持
穩健，內需仍為經濟成長提供支撐，但通膨壓力較前月略有升溫，物價回落至聯
準會 2%目標之過程仍具挑戰，後續仍須持續關注能源價格變化、核心通膨走勢
及消費動能對聯準會貨幣政策方向之影響。 
聯準會(Fed)於 2026 年 10 月 FOMC 會議以 10 比 2 表決結果，決定將聯邦基
金利率目標區間維持於 3.75%至 4.00%不變，符合市場預期；謹將本次會議聲明
及主席 Kevin Warsh 記者會重點摘錄如下：(一)聯準會認為美國經濟活動持續以
強勁步調擴張，較前次聲明所稱之「穩健擴張」進一步上修對經濟表現之評價；
儘管地緣政治情勢使經濟前景不確定性仍偏高，但國內支出維持韌性，生產力成
長與資本投資表現強勁，就業增幅大致與勞動力成長同步，失業率變動不大，顯
示整體經濟基本面仍具相當韌性；(二)通膨仍高於聯準會 2%目標，委員會表示需
累積更多經濟與物價資訊，以評估適當政策立場，確保通膨適時回歸 2%目標，
並重申將致力維持物價穩定 ；(三)本次決議出現兩張反對票 ， 聯準會理事Lisa Cook
及克里夫蘭聯邦準備銀行總裁 Beth Hammack 主張升息 1 碼，反映部分委員對通
膨風險仍抱持較為謹慎態度；(四)主席 Kevin Warsh 於記者會表示，近期聯邦公
開市場委員會討論焦點多集中於物價穩定，特別是在近期能源及相關價格再度上
揚情況下，聯準會有責任避免個別產業價格上漲擴散至整體經濟，並確保通膨預
期維持穩定；(五)Warsh 並宣布調整 FOMC 會議安排，2027 年仍將維持每年 8 次
會議，但其中 1 次將改為專門討論貨幣政策策略之會議，不進行利率表決或發布
政策聲明，並預期 2028 年將有 2 次會議採取此模式，以騰出更多時間進行長期
政策與重大議題研議。整體而言，本次會議雖維持利率不變，但聯準會仍認為通
膨問題尚未完全解決，後續政策方向仍將取決於通膨、勞動市場及整體經濟數據
表現。

31%略為回升；在國會選舉政黨支持度方面， 47%的登記選民表示傾向支持民主

二、歐元區—歐洲央行如期暫停升息，12 月升息機率較高 
 歐元區第三季 GDP 季增率為 0.4%，但有部份是受到愛爾蘭經濟成長率大幅
波動的扭曲，若剔除愛爾蘭，則歐元區第三季 GDP 季增率僅約 0.2%。該季受到
物價壓力上升 、 地緣政治緊張局勢等因素影響 ， 對消費及投資需求構成負面因素 ，
但世足賽以及德國財政支出擴張的支持下，仍使歐元區經濟保持正向表現。就國
別而言，德、法、義、西GDP 季增率分別為 1.2%、-0.1%、0.8%、2.7%；其中德
國經濟活動受到財政擴張的提振；法國的消費支出雖強勁，但受到投資減緩和進
口增加拖累；義大利製造業產出萎縮拖累成長表現；西班牙在住宅營建熱潮的支
持下， 經濟活動持續優於其他國家， 但歐盟NGEU 計畫到期以及政府財政整頓力
道，西班牙後續經濟動能可能會逐漸放緩。 
 歐洲央行在 10 月 29 日宣布維持政策利率不變，主要是近期歐元區各國殖利
率普遍上升 ， 對經濟造成限制性因素 ， 且不確定性可能會隨著時間的推移而累積 ，
從而拖累經濟成長。不過，歐洲央行仍認為近期高漲的能源價格對通膨的壓力相
當大，使央行考慮進一步採取貨幣政策措施因應。因此，除非經濟成長受到超出
預期的衝擊、能源價格下跌或金融環境急劇收緊，一般預期歐洲央行在 12 月的
政策會議將會宣布升息一碼。

逐步回補。展望未來，製造商預估 10 月及 11 月產量將分別月增 5.1%及 0.8%，

五、印度及東南亞—泰、菲進出口創歷史新高，泰國央行按兵不動以支撐經濟 
(1) 新加坡：9 月消費者物價指數(CPI)年增率小幅回落至 2.2%、核心通膨率自 8
月的 2.2%降至 2%，反映交通運輸（7.4%） 、服裝鞋帽（2.1%）及家庭耐用品
和服務 （0.2%）價格漲幅顯著放緩，此外，由於行業競爭壓力，資訊通信技術
服務價格的進一步回落也抑制整體服務價格的上漲。隨著 11 月電力和天然氣
價格下調生效，預計 2026 年第四季核心通膨將進一步放緩。 
(2) 泰國：9 月出口額達 384.4 億美元、年增 24.0%，創歷史新高，主要得益於工
業產品 出口成長 25.4%，其中， 以電腦及相關設備（ 59.4%）、電話及零件
（117.5%）及電路板（45.9%）成長最為顯著，對美國出口額年增 50.1%，對
中國和日本的出口額分別成長 18.4%和 19.0%。9 月進口額 443 億美元、年增
50.4%，創歷史新高，因出口導向製造業生產需求及全球能源價格的上漲，主
要類別進口額均有所成長，其中，資本貨物(電腦、機械零件、電路板等)進口
大增 92.3%，燃料、原材料和中間產品進口增幅均逾 4 成、運輸設備在飛機與
航空設備進口大增 12 倍下成長 24%。9 月貿易逆差大幅擴大至 59 億美元，累
計 1 至 9 月 ， 貿易逆差達 437 億美元 ， 出口額成長19.6%、進口額成長 37.8%。
泰國央行 10 月會議維持基準利率於 1%不變，維持寬鬆的貨幣政策立場，以
支持經濟復甦的不平衡 。經濟預估方面 ，強於預期的出口以及與科技和人工智
慧週期相關的投資，預估 2026 年和 2027 年經濟成長率將分別成長 2.4%和
2.1%，然而，高額的家庭債務、脆弱的勞動市場和疲軟的購買力繼續抑制私人
消費，使經濟成長仍低於潛在水平。通膨方面，受能源價格上漲和聖嬰現象的
影響，預計通膨率在 2027 年初將出現短暫上升，之後隨著國內需求的疲軟而
回落，2026 年和 2027 年整體通膨率平均分別為 2.1%和 1.3%。

用相關內容。

9

(3) 菲律賓:9 月出口額達 98.7 億美元、 年增35.6%，主要由電子產品出口成長 72%
所帶動，占總出口額達 70.1%，其中半導體元件、辦公設備、電子設備及零件
出口成長分別達 86%、89%、73.5%，對美國出口大增 135%(26.3 億美元、占
總出口 26.6%)，對台灣出口成長 36.8%，對越南、新加坡分別成長 488%及
30%。9 月進口額達 150.3 億美元、年增 25.7%，創歷史新高，主要係電子產
品進口成長 87.4%(半導體 144%)，礦物燃料和潤滑油 、 穀物及穀物製品進口亦
分別成長 53.3%、22.8%，韓國是主要進口來源國、占進口總額 26.1%，其次
為中國占 22.3%。進出口相抵，9 月貿易逆差擴大至 51.5 億美元，累計 1 至 9
月 ， 貿易逆差從去年同期的376 億美元擴大至 472 億美元，出口額成長 17.2%、
進口額成長 20.3%。 
六、台灣—AI 動能續強，經濟成長動能穩健 
9 月景氣對策信號續呈紅燈，綜合判斷分數為 41 分，較上月增加 1 分，燈號
續呈紅燈，其中工業及服務業加班工時，受惠 AI 相關產業生產動能維持熱絡，
由綠燈轉呈黃紅燈，其餘八項燈號均維持不變。另領先與同時指標皆呈上升，顯
示國內經濟動能維持穩健。 
主計總處 10 月 30 日公布台灣第三季經濟成長率為 14.36%，主要受 AI 需求
強勁帶動出口及企業投資大幅成長，民間消費亦維持熱絡，顯示外需動能正逐步
向內需擴散，整體經濟呈現內外皆熱。展望未來，AI 需求、半導體相關投資及企
業資本支出持續擴張，加上年底消費旺季，可望支撐短期景氣表現；惟今年經濟
高速成長墊高比較基期，明年增速料將放緩，後續仍須關注 AI 需求延續性、成
長動能向傳統產業擴散情形及金融市場波動等不確定因素。

壹、訪 談／法 說 內 容 
重點摘要： 
 既有調價與 AI 產品出貨增加，帶動 3Q26 毛利率升至 41.0% 、
EPS 達 5.83 元；第四季雖為消費性淡季，公司仍指引營收、
毛利率及營益率小幅季增，呈現淡季不淡。  
 3Q26 AI 相關應用占營收 19.8% ，較前季 16% 提高 3.8 個百分
點， MLCC 、鉭電容與電源端產品共同帶動成長。第三季訂單
出貨比仍接近 2，公司規劃 2027 年 MLCC 及鉭電容擴產 10%
至 15% ，自第二季及下半年分批開出。  
 預估 2026 ／2027 年營收 1,841.63 ／2,498.39 億元 ，YoY 增長
38.5% ／35.7% ，EPS 為 20.68 ／35.71 元。 2027 年獲利成長
來自既有調價全年化、稼動率升至滿載、擴產及高階產品組合
提升。

一、 AI 需求帶動，第四季營收與獲利率續增  
3Q26 營收 495.32 億元， QoQ 增長 11.4% 、YoY 增長 49.7% ，連續
第七季季增，創下單季營收歷史新高。價格調整逐步反映，加上
AI 產品組合與營運效率改善，毛利率達 41.0% ，較前季提高 2.5
個百分點；營業利益率 29.5% ，EPS 為 5.83 元。投入成本提高抵
銷部分效益，但營收與毛利成長仍帶動獲利增加。

AI 相關應用占營收已由 1Q26 的 15%、2Q26 的 16%， 提高至3Q26
的 19.8% 。按公布占比計算，第三季 AI 相關營收約 98.07 億元，
較第二季約 71.13 億元增長 37.9% 。成長涵蓋 MLCC 、鉭電容，以
及電源端使用的薄膜與鋁電容，反映運算、通訊與供電需求共同
增加。工業營收亦已連續七季成長，車用維持正向，消費性營收
仍有成長，但速度低於其他應用。

第四季通常為消費性產品淡季，公司考量接單及在手訂單仍強，
指引營收、毛利率與營業利益率均較第三季小幅提高。本研究採
稼動率約 95% ，以既有調價遞延反映支撐淡季營收，預估 4Q26
營收 520.09 億元， QoQ 增長 5.0% 、YoY 增長 44.6% ，毛利率
42.78% 、營業利益率 31.43% 、EPS 為 6.36 元。

二、稼動率接近滿載，新增產能 2Q27 起貢獻  
3Q26 標準型與利基型產品稼動率均接近 95% ，公司預期 4Q26 超
過 95% ，並透過去瓶頸、效率改善與逐步擴充產能支應訂單。第

三季營收已較前季增加 11.4% ，訂單出貨比仍接近 2，顯示較高出
貨基期下接單維持高水準 ， 尤其MLCC 與鉭電容等 AI 相關產品需
求較強。

公司規劃 2027 年 MLCC 及鉭電容產能較目前增加 10% 至 15% ，
部分於第二季、部分於下半年開出。此為相對目前產能的整體增
幅，新增產能須隨設備交付、驗證與量產進度反映；公司亦表示，
擴產後希望稼動率回到 85% 至 90% 的健康水準。

三、漲價分批反映， 2027 年獲利仍以電容產品為主  
國巨 2026 年 2 月調高晶片電阻價格 10% 至 20%， 並同步調整鉭電
容； 7 月調價範圍擴至各產品線，假設幅度約 15% 至 25% ，並涵
蓋 EMS 與 OEM 直接客戶，通常延後一至兩季反映，因此第三季
已認列部分效益， 10 月新一季價格生效。

MLCC 與鉭電容為目前成長最明確的產品。第三季 MLCC 占營收
21.5% ，高於前季 19.1% ；鉭電容占 23.7% ，營收季增 10.0% 、年
增 45.3%。 公司表示鉭粉價格自年初趨於穩定 ， 已進行策略採購及
長期供應鏈布局，支持後續供貨與成本管理。

四、預估 2026 ／2027 年 EPS 為 20.68 ／35.71 元 
預估 2026 ／2027 年營收 1,841.63 ／2,498.39 億元 ，YoY 增長 38.5%
／35.7% ，EPS 為 20.68 ／35.71 元。2027 年隨既有調價全年反映、
假設稼動率提高至 100%，以 及MLCC 與鉭電容新增產能自第二季
起分批貢獻，帶動營收與獲利成長。

五、投資建議：買進，目標價 892 元 
參考國外 MLCC 大廠本益比，以及國巨 AI 佔比持續提高，給予
25 倍本益比，以 2027 年 EPS 計算 35.71 ，目標價 892 元，投資建
議為買進。

貳、產 業 及 營 運 概 況 
一、完整被動元件產品線， AI 相關由板端延伸至電源  
國巨透過產品開發及 Pulse 、KEMET 、奇力新等併購布局，產品涵
蓋 MLCC 、鉭電容、鋁電容、薄膜電容、電阻、磁性元件與感測
器，服務工業、運算、通訊、車用及消費性市場。 AI 設備從機房
供電、機櫃電源轉換到運算板，都需要處理電壓穩定、濾波、儲
能及訊號干擾；不同位置的電壓、空間與可靠度要求不同，使完
整產品組合有利於參與客戶多個供電環節。

3 
公司第三季 AI 相關應用占比接近兩成，已分布於多項產品線。隨
運算功耗提高，成長範圍由靠近 GPU 與 ASIC 的板端電容，擴大
至通訊設備、電源模組及機櫃供電。磁性元件可參與電源轉換及
濾波，電阻用於訊號與電流相關功能，感測產品則提供溫度等監
測需求。

供應能力也逐漸成為競爭條件。國巨的多產品、全球製造據點及
既有客戶關係，有助協調產品開發與後續供貨； TDK 與太陽誘電
討論先進 MLCC 、電感的共同開發及製造、採購合作，亦反映高
容值、高可靠度與穩定供應的重要性提高。

二、MLCC 與鉭電容為主軸，高容值與穩定性優勢擴大  
AI 運算功耗與供電密度增加，要求電容在有限空間內提供足夠電
容量，並維持供電穩定。 MLCC 具備小型化與高密度配置的優勢，
對高容值、小尺寸與可靠度的要求隨平台升級提高；鉭電容則具
備高容值與電容量穩定性優勢，可配合運算及通訊設備的不同低
壓電源設計，支應儲能與濾波需求。兩者實際搭配取決於電路位
置、電壓、體積與成本。

國巨透過 KEMET 具備鉭電容與多類電容產品布局，可配合客戶
選擇不同供電方案。當高容值 MLCC 供應吃緊或設計成本提高
時，其他電容可提供設計選項，但轉換仍須完成電路調整與驗證，
不能將短缺直接換算成鉭電容新增訂單。本研究以目前已觀察到
的產品成長、擴產與價格假設推估， 2027 年 MLCC 及鉭電容營收
分別為 628.36 億元及 669.79 億元， YoY 增長 65.6% 及 51.1% ，合
計營收占比由 2026 年的 44.67% 升至 51.96% 。

2026 年就達到滿載」

根據 Future Electronics 最新資料亦顯示交期延長 。 村田低於1μF
產品交期由 9 月 26–28 週延長至 10 月 30–36 週，高於 1μF 及
車規產品由 26–30 週延長至 30–36 週； TDK 低於 1μF 產品由
26–36 週延長至 30–40 週，高 於1μF 及車規產品由 26–48 週調
整至 30–48 週。三星電機上述三類產品均由 30 週以上調整至 52
週，價格維持向上。國巨低於 1μF、高於 1μF 及車規產品交期
均維持 30–52 週，交期與價格趨勢持續向上，整體而言， AI 伺服
器與電源規格升級持續帶動被動元件需求，交期不僅維持高檔，
主要 MLCC 廠及部分電阻產品更進一步拉長，漲價品項亦有增
加，延續供不應求的正向看法。

5 
高效能晶片的 AI 伺服器中不可或缺的元件。隨著大型科技公司準
備明年推出下一代 AI 伺服器，三星電機預計 AI 用 MLCC 的需求
將達到今年的約八倍 ， 這項預測較上一季公佈的超七倍有所上調。

國巨本次法說表示，全球代理商庫存在過去三個月由約五個月降
至四個月，大中華區代理商維持兩個月以下。供應商接單偏強、
交期延長與通路庫存下降相互呼應，增加客戶提前確保供應的需
求。

四、滿載提高既有產出， 2027 年擴產分批支應訂單

本研究預期 2027 年高階電容供需仍偏緊，主要考量需求成長、產
品升級與新增供給之間存在時間差。 AI 平台提升功耗與供電密
度，對電容量及小型化提出更高要求；高階產品也會改變產能配
置，設備投入增加未必等比例轉化為可交付的標準品顆數。三星
規劃菲律賓新廠於 2027 年量產高附加價值 MLCC，同時仍預期高
階供應偏緊，村田將兩年擴產 10-15% 計畫考慮提前，皆是供不應
求現象。

國巨新增產能自第二季及下半年陸續開出，第一季主要仍依靠既
有產能與去瓶頸。本研究採 MLCC 、鉭電容年底有效產能較目前
增加 15% ，全年平均增幅為 7.5% 。因此，年底產能增加 15% 不等
於全年供給增加 15%； 加上第三季訂單出貨比仍接近 2， 新增供給
需先支應既有接單與後續需求持續。

基於交期延長、高階產品需求增加及擴產分批到位，本研究假設
國巨於 2027 年針對 MLCC 及鉭電容有機會再調價 ， 根據今年國巨
2026 年 2 月調高晶片電阻價格 10% 至 20% ，並同步調整鉭電容；
7 月調價範圍擴至各產品線，假設幅度約 15% 至 25% ，先假設明
年特定產品再調升 15% ，並假設於下半年完整反應。此輪假設調
價僅涵蓋兩項電容，屬同規格售價提高，與既有升規組合及 2026
年調價全年化分開計算 。(註：15% 幅度及生效時點為本研究假設，
並非公司已公布指引。 )

成本方面，公司表示鉭粉價格已趨穩定，且已進行策略採購，支
持供貨及成本管理。

叁、業 績 及 獲 利 預 估

二、預估 2027 年營收 2,498.39 億元， EPS 為 35.71 元 
2027 年各季維持 100% 稼動率 ，MLCC 與鉭電容有效產能自第二季
起逐步增加 ， 並沿用高階產品組合提升的假設 。 第一季營收562.55
億元， QoQ 增長 8.2% ，反映稼動率由 95% 升至 100% 、調價剩餘
效益及產品組合提升。另假設第二季僅 MLCC 及鉭電容再調價
15%，第三季起完整反映，第四季維持新價格，貢獻全年新增營收
96.39 億元。第二至第四季營收分別為 587.39 、659.50 、688.95 億
元，全 年 營 收2,498.39 億元 ，YoY 增長 35.7%。 預估毛利率47.09%、
營業費用 247 億元、費用率 9.89% ，營業利益 929.55 億元， YoY
增長 77.7% ；業外淨收入 18.60 億元、基本稅率 22% ，全年 EPS
為 35.71 元， YoY 增長 72.7% 。

Realtek
Company description
Founded in 1987, Realtek is a leading fabless semiconductor with
headquarters in Hsinchu, Taiwan. It has an extensive global footprint with
operational offices in China, Japan, Korea, Singapore, and the US. Realtek's
product portfolio is well-diversified; generally, it can be categorized into three
business groups: Communications Network (Wi-Fi, Bluetooth, automotive
Ethernet, PC Ethernet, switch, etc.), Computer Peripherals (audio codec,

clock IC, card reader, IP cam SoC, USB  hub controller, Type-C controller, SSD
controller) and Multimedia (TV SoC, LCD controller, STB SoC, etc.).
Investment strategy
We rate the shares Buy. We believe Realtek is well-positioned to ride on
continued specs upgrade demand for WiFi, switch and 10G PON as well as the
strong advancements in the automotive and Io T sectors. In the long term, we
believe Realtek's cost-effective, high-performance, and comprehensive wired

and wireless solutions should become more critical with the growing
connectivity requirement at the edge.
Valuation
Our target price for Realtek is set at NT$900 based on our target PE multiple
of 20x to our 2027 EPS. We believe our PER of 20x, higher than its 3-year
forward PER average, is justified by its solid growth opportunity in automotive
and switch/PON business. We also believe its continued efforts in products
mix optimization should help to support its margins expansion. At our target

price, the shares would trade at a 2026/27E P/B of 8x/6x.
Risks
Key downside risks to our target price include: 1) Slowdown in technology
migration for its networking business; 2) Weaker-than-expected growth
momentum for automotive Ethernet sales; 3) Worse-than-expected PC/NB
and consumer electronic demand; 4) Slower-than-expected GM expansion
due to pricing competition from peers; and 5) Higher-than-expected memory
cost burden to dampen the order outlook from its networking and PC/NB

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do
not consider No View to be a recommendation. For all Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside calls, risk exists that the
catalyst(s) and associated share-price movement will not materialize as expected.

## Document 108: 261029_daiwa_Win Semiconductors

Source PDF: all report pdf/261029_daiwa_Win Semiconductors.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-10-29
Pages: 7
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Taiwan Information Technology

What’s new: Following our results memo, we raise our forecasts and TP 
for Win Semiconductors (WinSemi), but lower our rating by one notch to 
Outperform (2) on valuation grounds. While its topline trajectory appears to 
be unfolding intact, its margin trajectory is running better than our 
expectations. This, together with management’s positive commentary 
across its key business lines of RF, infrastructure and optics, reinforces our 
thesis that 3 growth pillars will turn around WinSemi’s bottom line and 
optimise its structural profitability (Upgrading: profit optimisation round the 
corner, 24 July). We suggest accumulating only on pullbacks.

What’s the impact: Forecast revisions. We raise our 2027-28E EPS by 
2-11% for WinSemi to factor in its higher margin trajectory, primarily on 
better product mix geared towards the infrastructure business, followed by 
the ramp-up in optics for AI optical communications (OC). We note the 
surge in our 2026E EPS revision is a result of the company’s non-operating 
gains of TWD684m in 3Q26, mainly from its 23%-owned ITEQ (6213 TT, 
TWD778, Buy [1], covered by Sheng Cheng), which distorts WinSemi’s 
growth trajectory at the bottom line into 2027.

Transformation paying off. At its results call, management commented on 
resilient RF demand from iOS and for WiFi routers, strong demand for low 
Earth orbit (LEO) satellite communications, and a continued ramp in pin 
diode (PD) sales after the product entered mass-production from late-
2Q26. This should be followed by laser diode (LD), such as continuous 
wave (CW) and electro-absorption modulated laser (EML), though 
customer qualifications will take time. All the commentary reinforces our 
thesis that 3 growth pillars will help turn around WinSemi’s bottom line for 
profitability optimisation after its business transformation: 1) RF business 
transformation paying off with premiumisation across cellular and WiFi for 
differentiation, 2) high-margin infrastructure scaling up on a robust LEO 
build, and 3) AI OC ramping fast, with broadened offerings from peripheral 
chips (optical drivers and modulators) to core chips for PD and LD.

What we recommend: Given our forecast revisions, we lift our 12M TP for 
WinSemi to TWD620 (previous: TWD449), based on a higher 4-quarter 
forward PER target of 55x (previous: 50x), justified by its 2025-28E EPS 
CAGR of 54% (previous: 50%) from a PEG perspective. Yet we lower our 
rating by one notch to Outperform (2) on valuation grounds, with key 
downside risk of commodity cost hikes worsening consumer demand.

How we differ: Our 2026E EPS is 40% above consensus due to our 
inclusion of the non-operating gains in 3Q26, while our 2027E EPS is 9% 
above likely on our higher margin assumptions.

29 October 2026

Downgrading: profit optimisation on the go

➢ Raising our forecasts on a higher margin trajectory 
➢ Growth pillars paying off across infrastructure, optics and RF 
➢ Downgrading our rating to Outperform (2), with a higher TP of TWD620

Company profile 
Founded in 1999, Win Semiconductors Corp (WinSemi) is the world’s largest compound semiconductor 
foundry, focusing on gallium-arsenide (GaAs) foundry services for customers in both wireless and wire-
line communication markets, as well as infrastructure applications. It has a diverse technology portfolio 
of processes that supports microwave frequency requirements from 50MHz to 100GHz, with 
applications including smartphones, tablet PCs, cellular base-stations, very small aperture terminals 
(VSAT) and fibre optics. It has started penetrating into vertical cavity surface emitting laser (VCSEL) 
foundries since 2017 for consumer laser applications such as 3D laser sensors and cameras.

Win Semiconductors (3105 TT): 29 October 2026 
ESG analysis

Update Date: 4 May 2026 
Source: Daiwa, Company

ESG risks
Risks Management Analyst comments
G
Executive/board
quality 2
WinSemi's board of directors (BoD) has 10 members, including 3 independent directors,
representing 30% of total directors. Among the BoD, three of its non-independent directors are
also executive management.
Capital management 2
Since 2017, its dividend payout ratio has remained above 58%, except for 2024 when no
dividend was paid. Despite being the largest compound semiconductor foundry in the world,
WinSemi is continually expanding its capacity for future growth. Thus, we see the balance
between investment and dividend as appropriate.
Related party &
transaction 1 It did not have any meaningful related-party transactions in 2023.
S Supply chain management 2
WinSemi We actively cooperate with local suppliers and improve their development capabilities,
reduce carbon emissions and create local employment opportunities. WinSemi also conducts on-
site audits of suppliers and annual assessment of suppliers on environmental protection and
safety & health management systems. In 2023, 100% of WinSemi's new suppliers signed the
"WIN Supplier Code of Conduct".'"
E Water & wastewater management 1
Effluent that flows out of WinSemi’s facilities will first undergo pre-treatment in-house and then
treatment by the wastewater treatment plant at Hwaya Technology Park before discharge. The
plant has an acid and alkali wastewater recycling system and chemical mechanical polishing
wastewater recycling system for wastewater management.
E
Waste & hazardous
materials
management
1
WinSemi has established a hazardous substance-free management procedure and also
collaborates with its suppliers to produce products that are hazardous substance-free for
customers and consumers. WinSemi specifies in purchase contracts and order forms with its
suppliers that the provided materials may not contain hazardous substances as defined by
WinSemi.
For waste management, it aims to increase the recycling rate of waste solvents and reduce the
use of raw materials in production.
E GHG emissions 1
For reduction of energy consumption, it prioritises high-efficiency and low energy consumption
models when replacing equipment and has also established annual energy conservation
management objectives. WinSemi targets to reduce electricity consumption (kWh/6"e wafer) by
12% from 2018 to 458.58 and PFCs emission (ton CO2e/6"e wafer) by 50% from 2018 to 0.114
in 2030.

Hon Precision (7769.TW): Large package and CPO on track to drive 2027 
content growth; 3Q26 beat; TP up to NT$12,600; Buy
29 October 2026 | 3:16AM HKT
Hon Precision delivered better-than-expected 3Q26 results, and highlighted: 1) 
product mix upgrade: a higher ASP for its large-package (Mega Tray) solution, with 
20-30% of some customers’ new orders already on Mega Tray, 2) CPO Insertion 4 
customer qualification expected to finish by 2026 year-end, with mass production 
targeted in 1Q27, 3) a reiterated capacity expansion target of +50% for 2027, and 4) 
new projects expected to lift GM from 2H27. We remain positive on Hon Precision 
and raise our 12m TP to NT$12,600 (implied 49x 2027E P/E vs. 83% NI YoY in 
2027-28E on avg.). Maintain Buy. 
ATC platforms across FT and SLT to meet customers’ needs 
During the meeting, management laid out its ATC roadmap in detail, covering three 
platforms. ATC 3 uses liquid cooling and is adopted by an ASIC customer, with fast 
temperature ramp, c.10kW of power (vs. market mainstream of 2-3kW) and a 
25-125°C range. ATC 5 uses refrigerant and supports tri-temp (-70-125°C) at 
c.10kW, mainly for automotive customers. ATC 8 uses refrigerant with c.25kW of 
power, for high-power applications. For large packages such as 200mm x 200mm, 
management plans to handle 12kW by end-2026 and 25kW by end-2027. Overall, 
Hon offers a different ATC system for each customer’s test requirements and installs 
it on its FT or SLT handlers. Management believes this ability to meet varying 
temperature and power needs is why customers favor Hon’s products. 
Mega Tray solution across FT and SLT supports a higher ASP uplift 
The large-package (Mega Tray) solution targets end-customer packages larger than 
120mm x 150mm and is backward-compatible with current chip sizes. Small-volume 
shipments start in 2H26 and penetration is anticipated to rise in 2027. In final test 
(FT), Hon offers the HP9050, a dual-temp model, and the HP1050, a tri-temp model, 
which can be paired with different ATC platforms depending on customer needs. In 
system-level test (SLT), Hon offers the HP-3036, which supports 36 test boards in 
parallel (vs. 6-12 today), with an ASP more than 2x current, as longer test times make 
parallel testing increasingly important. Management emphasized the importance of 
integrating full automation and advanced AOI into these new handlers. The handlers 
can also be paired with Autonomous Mobile Robots (AMRs), which OSAT customers 
are actively adopting. On ASP , Mega Tray enjoys better price vs. the mainstream 
handler solution. For some customers, 20-30% of new orders are already on Mega 
Tray.

### Page 2

CPO qualification by 2026 year-end 
Management said its CPO Insertion 4 tool has been ready for mass production since 
end-October, with current shipments mainly small-volume engineering tools. Customer 
qualification starts in November and is expected to finish in December. The customer 
targets mass production in 1Q27, and Hon has prepared long lead time materials in 
advance. 
The tool tests simultaneously for optical and electrical performance. This requires two 
thermal systems, one for the ASIC and a separate system for the OEs, while ATE handles 
the electrical test. Hon’s in-house optical alignment stage is still under qualification. 
For CPC, Hon is focused on mechanical alignment. Some customers have strong demand 
and are looking to complete NPI as soon as possible. Hon is co-developing with a US 
networking customer and a major ATE vendor. 
2027 capacity expansion reiterated 
Looking into 2027, management reiterated its capacity expansion target of +50%. With 
order visibility for the next 2-3 quarters, we expect revenue to keep growing into 1H27. 
On margins, high-end ATC and Mega Tray shipments start in 4Q26, and CPO tools begin 
delivery in 1Q27. With a 3-6 month lag for equipment revenue recognition, management 
expects these new products to drive GM higher in 2H27. 
On competition, management said major OSATs currently mainly use Hon’s equipment, 
and challengers are mostly at an early stage. Management sees its advantage in ATC 
technology, where it leads peers, as well as a more complete product portfolio given 
widely varying customer requirements. On CPO, management sees its optical and 
electrical co-testing tool as next-generation equipment, leading in the market. 
Earnings revision and Valuation

3Q26 beat: Hon Precision’s 3Q26 revenue of NT$16.2bn (+17.4% QoQ, +97.5% YoY) 
came in 5.0% above GSe and 7.4% above Bloomberg consensus. Margin-wise, 3Q26 GM 
of 56.9% (vs. 56.1% in 2Q26) was in line with GSe/cons of 56.5%/57.0%. For opex, the 
company’s 3Q26 opex ratio of 7.3% was higher vs. GSe/cons of 5.6%/6.6%, primarily 
due to higher R&D expense. As a result, 3Q26 EPS of NT$37.33 came in 4.7% above GSe 
of NT$35.64 and 8.0% above cons of NT$34.61.

Earnings revision: We factor in 3Q26 results and raise our EPS by 2% / 2% / 1% in 
2026-28E mainly on higher revenues and GM, reflecting the better-than-expected 3Q26

Price Target Risks and Methodology - Hon Precision (7769.TW) 
Valuation: We are Buy rated on Hon Precision. Our 12-month TP of NT$12,600 is based 
on a target P/E of 32x (based on close peer correlation analysis) applied to our 2028E 
EPS, and discounted back to 2027E at 13.6% CoE. Our key assumptions for CoE include: 
(1) 1.5x beta, (2) a 4.25% risk-free rate, and (3) a market risk premium at 6.25%. 
Key downside risks to our views: (1) softer AI/HPC demand, (2) slower adoption of SLT 
among AI ASICs, and (3) intensifying competition.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.

https://www.gs.com/research/hedge.html.

3Q26 earnings beat on better margins: See Results Summary
4Q26 revenue seen rising QoQ: Management guided for  4Q26 revenue to grow
QoQ, driven by power related products in Cloud & AIoT. IT/CE should continue to
decline YoY, despite strong growth from the LEO (low earth orbit). Optoelectronics
should grow by 5-10% YoY. Overall, we estimate revenue will grow by 5% QoQ in
4Q26 and GM will expand further to 26.1% (+0.2ppt QoQ) on a more favorable

revenue mix.
2027 expected to be another solid year: Looking to 2027, management holds an
optimistic view, guiding for the Cloud & AIoT business to grow by ~70% YoY,
Optoelectronics to grow by ~10% YoY and IT/CE business to stay roughly flat YoY.
AI remains the major driver, and should benefit from the growing AI deployment and
the spec upgrade of power-related products.
800VDC power rack under qualification: Lite-On sent its 800VDC power rack for

qualification in August and will have the next round of integrated system
qualification in November, aiming to be MP ready in 1Q27. That said, shipments will
depend upon customer deployment plans. Management expects only small volume
shipments in 1H27, and said visibility into the adoption in the new generation ASIC/
GPU platform from 2H27 remains to be seen, consistent with the consensus that
HVDC is more of a 2028 story at the earliest.
More offerings in the pipeline: Management sent DC/DC module samples to CSP

for qualification and expects to see some revenue contribution from 3Q27 (not
meaningful, though). Its 120KW CDU has obtained qualification in 3Q26 and should
see more volume from 4Q26, with the next generation 220KW CDU currently in the
development stage. In September 2026, it invested in a 25% stake in DCX, a liquid
cooling solution supplier in Poland, to strengthen its capability in the cooling space .
Stay EW: We think near-term sentiment should be relatively positive given the solid

3Q26 results, especially after the recent share price correction. Looking ahead, we
think Lite-On will grow along with the AI capex cycle, driven by its power-related
offerings, though execution and progress with new customers is worth monitoring.

Results Summary
3Q26 beat on better margins
Off revenue of NT$57,403mn (+9% QoQ/+28% YoY), GM contracted by 1.3ppt QoQ to
25.9% (vs. MSe/consensus at 24.6%/25.4%). The sequential decline was due to a high base
in 2Q26, which was elevated due to some one-offs. Opex was well controlled at NT
$6,211mn (opex ratio 10.8%), lower than our estimate of NT$6,851mn (opex ratio 11.5%)
and consensus of NT$7,097mn (opex ratio 11.8%)
Operating profit came in at NT$8,655mn (+6% QoQ/+85% YoY; OPM 15.1%),11% and 5%

higher than MSe of NT$7,807mn (OPM 13.1%) and consensus of NT$8,210mn (OPM
13.6%), respectively.
With non-op gains of NT$1,142mn (including FX gain of NT$734mn), net profit was NT
$7,429mn (+4% QoQ/+60% YoY; EPS NT$3.26), 18% and 11% above MSe (NT$6,313mn;
EPS NT$2.77) and consensus (NT$6,717mn; EPS NT$2.92).

Update
6
Valuation Methodology
Our price target (base case scenario value) is NT$285 (prior NT$315), derived from 15x
2027-28e P/E (previously 17x): We use price/earnings as our valuation methodology
because we believe it appropriately captures Lite-On's earnings growth potential. We use
a lower target multiple to reflect more conservative sentiment toward the AI space in the
near term. We believe our target P/E multiple of 15x is justified by an earnings CAGR of

50% seen over 2025-28, which implies a PEG of 0.3. This is lower than the average of 1.6
in 2020-25, when the stock was trading at an average P/E of 14x for a 9% earnings CAGR.
Bull case scenario value – NT$490 (previously NT$500): We apply a 25x P/E multiple
(previously 27x) to our average base case estimate for  2027-28. This scenario assumes
better-than-expected margin expansion, driven by operating efficiency and accelerating
production automation, while new business revenue comes in earlier than expected.

Strength from cloud computing and auto electronics is greater than expected.
Bear case scenario value – NT$190 (previously NT$200):  We apply a 10x P/E multiple
(previously 11x) to our average base case estimate for  2027-28. This scenario assumes
weaker-than-expected operating efficiency improvement, while pricing competition

STOCK RATINGS
Morgan Stanley uses a relative rating system using terms such as Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated or Underweight (see definitions below). Morgan Stanley does not assign ratings of Buy,
Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

Greater China Technology Hardware
Asia Pacific
Industry View In-Line
(-) PC OEMs and ODMs: AMD (covered by Joseph Moore) mentioned that PC sell-
through slowed down in late 3Q, particularly in the consumer segment, and that it
estimates PC units will fall in double digits (%) in CY27. This aligns with Morgan

Stanley's estimate at a ~11% y/y decline (report link). AMD also mentioned that it
expects to outgrow the market in CY27.
(+) Server OEMs, ODMs, and component makers: AMD expects CPU TAM to reach
US$220bn by 2030, implying a ~50% CAGR. It also mentioned that its Venice CPU
began shipments in 3Q and cloud providers are deploying it in 4Q.
(+) ABF substrates:  AMD said that it has secured the wafers, substrates, advanced
packaging, and memory needed for much higher 2027 growth, and is planning to add

more. It also mentioned that its supply chain is quite "fungible" across its PC and
server business, but overall it still "has more demand than it has supply."

Key Takeaways
We continue to view negative PC comments as bearish for OEMs and ODMs
positioned in the lower-end consumer segment, like Acer and Compal.
Strong  server CPU unit growth from AMD would be positive for Lotes, Wiwynn,
Unimicron, and NYPCB...
...and indirectly benefit GCE, EMC, TUC, and Co-Tech.
We see positive implications for Lotes if AMD takes share in both the PC and

Server segments.

Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.

## Document 235: [012450] 한화에어로스페이스(012450) 3Q26_ 그래도 믿는다한화에어로스페이스(012450) 3Q26_ 그래도 믿는다3Q26_ 그래도 믿는다 - 기업

Source PDF: all report pdf/[012450] 한화에어로스페이스(012450) 3Q26_ 그래도 믿는다한화에어로스페이스(012450) 3Q26_ 그래도 믿는다3Q26_ 그래도 믿는다 - 기업.pdf
Report source: Hankyung
Date: 2026-10-30
Pages: 5
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

2026.10.30   
한화에어로스페이스  
(012450)

3Q26: 그래도 믿는다  Company Brief

3분기 매출 6조 943억원/영업이익 4,520억원 
한화에어로스페이스의 3분기 매출은 6조 943억원/영업이익 4,520억원(OPM 7.4%)로 
컨센서스와 당사 추정치를 모두 대폭 하회하는 실적을 기록하였다. 앞서 실적을 발표한 
한화오션의 실적 쇼크를 감안하더라도, 주력사업인 지상방산 부문의 실적이 매출 1조 
2,455억원/영업이익 217억원(1.7%)로 지난 분기(2조 1,075억원/ 5,330억원/ 25.3%) 
는 물론 전년동기(2조 1,098억원/5,725억원/27.1%)보다도 현저히 감소하였다. 
일회성 요인은 대전사업장 폭발사고에 따른 복구비용 등 300억원, 일부 국내사업 
충당금 160억원 및 환율하락에 따른 효과 등이 있었으나 역시 이를 감안해도 내수와 
수출 모두 실질 이익률은 5%미만으로 추정된다. 지상방산 분기 매출은 1분기(1조 
2,211억)보다 오히려 소폭 증가하였으나 영업이익이 1분기 2,087억원에서 
217억원으로 대폭 감소한 점에 대하여 회사는 프로젝트별 믹스 구성에 따른 낮은 
이익률의 인도분이 반영되었다고 설명하고 있으나, 그럼에도 불구하고 수출과 내수 
이익률 모두 BEP에 가까웠다는 점은 쉽게 이해가 가지는 않는다. 다만 방산기업의 
특성상 대전사업장 사고에 따른 실제 납기 영향 등 회사가 공개할 수 없는 사정이 
있었을 것으로 추측할 뿐이다. 대전사업장은 현재 복구작업이 진행 중이며 27년 
상반기 중에는 가동 정상화할 것으로 예상된다.  
긍정적인 소식도 있다. 폴란드향 천무의 연간 인도대수가 기존 가이던스인 40대에서 
60대로 증가하였으며, 이를 포함하여 3분기에 인도되지 않은 물량의 대부분이 
4분기에 집중될 예정으로, 올해의 4분기 실적 개선폭은 통상적으로 볼 수 있는 
4분기의 계절적 집중도보다 더 커질 것으로 예상한다. 방산기업의 실적은 인도일정에 
따라 크게 변할 수 있는 분기 실적보다 연간 합산이 더 중요하다는 점을 한 번 더 
믿고, 4분기를 기다리는 게 좋겠다.

투자의견 BUY 유지, 목표주가 1,200,000원 하향  
투자의견 BUY를 유지하지만, SOTP기반 목표주가는 기존 1,490,000원에서 
1,200,000원으로 19.5% 하향한다. 비영업가치 중 자회사 한화시스템과 한화오션, 
쎄트렉아이의 지분가치 하락(합산 8.7조원→7.1조원), 그리고 지난 분기 대비 늘어난 
별도 순차입금(3.25조→4.95조원)을 반영하였으며 최근 방산섹터 전반의 밸류 하락을 
감안하여 영업가치 중 지상방산+항공우주의 Target P/E를 기존 35배에서 30배로 
조정하였다. 
3분기는 여러모로 악재가 겹쳤다. 주식시장 전반의 하락에 더해 방산섹터 수주 지연에 
따른 디레이팅, 그리고 납기 지연에 따른 실적 하락 등 나올 수 있는 악재는 다 
나왔다고도 볼 수 있다. 그러나 뒤집어보면 지연중인 수주는 시간의 문제일 뿐 가시화 
가능성이 매우 높으며, 실적 또한 진행률 인식이 아닌 인도기준 인식에 따른 일시적 
현상으로 4분기 이후 복구될 가능성이 크다. 펀더멘털의 유의미한 변화는 없다고 볼 
수 있으며 밸류에이션 매력도가 증가한 현 시점에서 추가 매수를 고려할 때다. 다만, 
대전사업장 사고에 따른 당국의 제재조치가 아직 확정되지 않았음은 잔여 리스크 
요인이다. 동종사의 신규계약 입찰제한 제재조치가 주가에 상당한 영향을 미쳤음을 
고려하면 한화에어로스페이스 또한 제재조치 여부를 지켜본 후 투자할 것을 권한다.  
Buy (Maintain)

 회사는 해당 종목을 1%이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다.  
 금융투자분석사와 그 배우자는 해당 기업의 주식을 보유하고 있지 않습니다.  
 당 보고서는 기관투자가 및 제 3자에게 E-mail등을 통하여 사전에 배포된 사실이 없습니다.  
 회사는 6개월간 해당 기업의 유가증권 발행과 관련 주관사로 참여하지 않았습니다. 
 당 보고서에 게재된 내용들은 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하고 있으며, 외부의 부당한 압력이나 간섭 없이 작성되었음을 확인합니다. 
 당 연구원은 한화에어로스페이스(012450)의 일부 비용을 지원받아 한화에어로스페이스 공장 투어(2026년 10월 1-2)를 다녀왔습니다. 
본 분석자료는 투자자의 증권투자를 돕기 위한 참고자료이며, 따라서, 본 자료에 의한 투자자의 투자결과에 대해 어떠한 목적의 증빙자료로도 사용될 수 없으며, 어떠한 경우에도 작성자 및 
당사의 허가 없이 전재, 복사 또는 대여될 수 없습니다. 무단전재 등으로 인한 분쟁발생시 법적 책임이 있음을 주지하시기 바랍니다.

## Document 272: AI交易松动，中国资产迎来机会！摩根大通也看好A股：还有10%-15%反弹空间

Source PDF: all report pdf/AI交易松动，中国资产迎来机会！摩根大通也看好A股：还有10%-15%反弹空间.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-11-05
Pages: 7
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

美国中期选举落地、全球 AI 交易松动之际，中国资产迎来新的配置窗⼝。摩根⼤通认为，
估值与仓位重置、政策托底及资⾦轮动将⽀撑 A 股进⼀步反弹，预计仍有 10%-15% 的上涨
空间，维持看好 A 股优于港股，重点关注境内科技股与券商。
摘要：
中国股市在美国中期选举落地后强势反弹，摩根⼤通认为，随着美国中期选举不确定性消散、
全球 AI 交易出现松动，中国资产正在迎来新的配置窗⼝。
11 ⽉ 5 ⽇， A 股三⼤指数震荡⾛⾼，创业板指盘中涨超 3% ，科创 50 ⼀度涨超 5% ，⾄午盘收盘均
有回落。光伏设备、贵⾦属、锂电池等板块领涨。此前的 4 ⽇， A 股亦⼤幅⾛强，沪深 300 上涨
2.3% ，创业板指涨 6.4% ，科创 50 劲升 9.9% ， A 股成交额时隔 25 个交易⽇重返 2 万亿元⼈⺠币。
据追⻛交易台消息，摩根⼤通策略师 Chloe Liu 、 Tim Huang 等⼈在最新报告中将此次反弹归因
于三⼤驱动因素：美国中期选举⻛险落地、估值与仓位⼤幅重置、以及国家队和监管机构⼊市

提振情绪。分析师指出，A 股估值距离 "924" 低点仅回升 8%-10% ，仍处于低位，预计有进⼀步
10%-15% 的反弹空间，并维持对 A 股优于港股的判断，重申看好境内科技股和券商板块。
⾼盛亚洲团队则在题为《中国：逢低买⼊？》的报告中写道，中国国内 AI 相关标的从夏季⾼点回
调近 54% 后， " 看起来越来越具备反弹条件 " ，并看好其 " 中国国内 AI 篮⼦ " （ GSXACADO ）。
中期选举落地，全球 AI 交易格局⽣变
美国中期选举结果出炉，市场不确定性随之消退。摩根⼤通认为，受⽴法约束与总统否决权限
制，此次选举结果不会对美国整体政策格局带来实质性改变，市场此前已对⺠主党横扫两院的
情形有所定价。历史数据显⽰，中国股票在美国中期选举结束后往往倾向于反弹，原因在于不
确定性的消散。
更值得关注的是，全球 AI 交易正出现松动迹象。德克萨斯州占美国 2030 年前规划数据中⼼容量
逾 25% ，⽽该州新届政府已释放出对新建数据中⼼采取更严格限制⽴场的信号。摩根⼤通指

出，随着全球 AI 交易争议升温，中国资产有望从中受益 —— 当前局⾯或引发空头回补及资⾦轮
动。
报告同时强调，中国股票本⾝可提供与全球 AI 交易的多元化配置价值，⽽中国 AI 的本⼟化发展有
其独⽴轨迹，对美国 AI 资本开⽀的依赖程度相对有限。
估值与仓位双重重置，反弹空间尚存
摩根⼤通认为，估值和仓位层⾯的全⾯重置，是⽀撑进⼀步反弹的重要结构性基础。
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

估 值⽅ ⾯ ， 即 便 经 历 11 ⽉ 4 ⽇ 的 上 涨 ， MSCI 中 国 和 沪 深 300 指数 仍 仅 较 "924" 低 点 ⾼ 出
8%-10% ，估值修复空间尚存。⾼盛⽅⾯数据则显⽰，其中国国内 AI 篮⼦的远期市盈率已从 7 ⽉
峰值时约 53 倍压缩⾄当前约 25 倍，回落⾄ 2025 年夏季 AI ⾏情启动前的⽔平，篮⼦指数较 7 ⽉初
⾼点累计下跌约 55% ，年内涨幅⼏近悉数回吐。
仓位⽅⾯，摩根⼤通列出三组数据：融资余额从 6 ⽉的 3 万亿元⼈⺠币回落⾄当前约 2.3 万亿元，
已退回⾄ 2025 年 9 ⽉⽔平，今年以来的杠杆积累基本消化；北向资⾦持有的 A 股 IT 板块敞⼝从 6 ⽉
末的 35% 降⾄ 9 ⽉末的 27% ；境内公募基⾦前⼗⼤重仓中， IT 持仓占⽐从 6 ⽉约 60% 降⾄ 9 ⽉
48% ，摩根⼤通估计 10 ⽉进⼀步降⾄约 40% 。这⼀仓位重置意味着较⼤规模的场外观望资⾦具
备⼊市潜⼒，有望推动科技板块的阶段性反弹。
⾼盛同样将仓位视为重要的结构性⽀撑。该⾏报告显⽰，全球主动基⾦对中国的配置持续下滑

⾄ 5.5% ，接近过去五年低点，处于 2015 年以来第 6 百分位，较 2020 年约 15% 的峰值配置缩⽔约
三分之⼆，相对基准低配 215 个基点。⾼盛分析师 Finn Dattenberg-Doyle 和 Janvi Sharma 在报
告中写道：
政策底部逐步夯实， " 国家队 " 是关键变量
在市场内⽣动⼒之外，政策层⾯的⽀撑同样不可忽视。科创 50 指数较 6 ⽉⾼点已累计回调约
50% ，并于 8 ⽉与美股 AI 反弹⾏情脱钩。进⼊ 10 ⽉后，国家队加速买⼊ ETF ，单⽉净买⼊规模达
3300 亿元⼈⺠币。下半年以来的 ETF 净流⼊规模，已相当于上半年净流出的 35% ，仍有约 9000
亿元⼈⺠币的潜在 " ⼲⽕药 " 。
本周⼈⺠银⾏与证监会释放的信号，进⼀步表明监管层有意为市场下⾏托底。摩根⼤通将此定
性为 " 不是⽕箭筒，但是更坚实的地板 " 。
市场营销反馈：关注点从 AI 向多元化板块扩散
摩根⼤通在⾹港和新加坡路演过程中收集到的投资者反馈显⽰，⼤多数投资者认同在政策托底

后下⾏空间有限，离岸投资者的增配意愿相对更强。
投资者的关注点正从 AI 向更多元化的板块扩散。银⾏股受到最多关注，其次是医疗健康，以及部
分提供可观股东回报和较⾼盈利能⻅度的消费类公司。
在个股层⾯，摩根⼤通重申旗下中国市场⼗⼤优选标的，同时新增⼀份 AI 相关股票筛选名单 ——
筛选条件为：摩根⼤通给予 " 增持 " 评级、 2027 年预期市盈率低于 20 倍、 2027 年预期每股盈利增
速超过 30% ，且股价较 52 周⾼点跌幅超 40% 。⾼盛则重点推介 GSXACADO 篮⼦，成分股覆盖拓
荆科技、⻓川科技、华虹半导体、中微公司、⻓鑫存储和寒武纪等。
" 从仓位⻆度来看，经历近期调整后，布局条件看起来更具建设性。 "

## Document 436: US Equity Views_ AI capex, required revenues, and what's priced in the AI trade

Source PDF: all report pdf/US Equity Views_ AI capex, required revenues, and what's priced in the AI trade.pdf
Report source: Goldman Sachs Publishing
Date: 2026-09-24
Pages: 16
Extraction status: ok

### Page 2

An update on AI and US equities

Both stocks and investor views relating to the AI trade have been exceptionally 
volatile during the last few months. Most of the volatility has been concentrated in the 
AI infrastructure complex, but the latest earnings season also drove sharp moves in the 
hyperscalers and among software stocks. 
Below we address three of the most common AI-related questions we have received 
from clients in recent weeks: 
1. What is the outlook for AI capex? 
2. What revenues are required to generate a sufficient return on recent AI 
investments? 
3. What outlook for AI is the equity market pricing?

1. What is the outlook for AI capex? 
The largest US hyperscalers are on track to spend $800 billion in capex in 2026 and 
consensus estimates imply they will spend $1.1 trillion in 2027. Consensus 2027 
capex estimates have been revised higher by $175 billion since the start of the Q2 
earnings season. Commentary from the hyperscalers during earnings season described a 
supply-constrained environment and large revenue backlogs, and these factors are 
driving the companies to tap both debt and equity markets to support additional 
investment spending. Based on consensus estimates, capex in 2027 is on track to reach a

### Page 3

larger share of GDP than any technological investment cycle since the railroad build-out 
in the late 1800’s. 
Our baseline forecast is that hyperscaler capex spending will increase substantially 
in 2027 but that the rate of both capex growth and upside surprises to consensus 
estimates will diminish relative to recent quarters. GS equity analysts estimate that 
hyperscaler capex will register $1.2 trillion in 2027 and $1.4 trillion in 2028. Their 
estimates are $130 billion and $100 billion above the consensus estimates, respectively, 
but nonetheless imply a deceleration in capex growth from nearly 100% in 2026 to 54% 
in 2027 and 12% in 2028.

The need for external financing and potential supply bottlenecks will likely 
constrain the rate of capex growth next year. Hyperscaler capex spending now 
exceeds cash flow from operations, which means large further growth will increasingly 
need funding via debt and equity issuance. While hyperscalers have added leverage, 
their balance sheets do not appear to be a binding constraint and their interest coverage 
ratios remain extremely healthy. Our credit strategists instead point to concentration in 
the investment grade market as the more relevant bottleneck, which will require 
additional funding from private credit and equity issuance. Potential government 
restrictions on data center construction or an intentional slowdown in frontier model 
roll-out could also act as potential constraints on spending, as could shortages of labor, 
power, memory, or other inputs. Constraints on capex represent a risk to the near-term 
earnings of AI infrastructure beneficiaries, but could help ease investor concerns about 
overbuilding.

### Page 4

Going forward, token demand growth will need to outpace declining token prices to 
support continued growth in investment spending. Frontier models are currently a 
key source of demand for hyperscaler compute. However, the rise of competitive 
open-source models has contributed to a decline in average token prices. Measures of 
frontier token demand slowed in July, simultaneous with sell-offs of many AI 
infrastructure stocks, but accelerated again in August. 
For most AI infrastructure companies, decelerating AI investment spending 
suggests decelerating EPS growth. AI infrastructure companies, and semis in 
particular, have been the clearest beneficiaries of the capex surge. Continued upward 
revisions to consensus 2027 capex estimates should support continued positive 
revisions to consensus 2027 EPS estimates for many AI infrastructure stocks, but the 
magnitude of revisions will likely diminish relative to what occurred in H1 2026.

### Page 5

2. What revenues are required to generate a return on AI investments? 
Our equity analysts estimate that the hyperscalers would need to generate annual 
AI revenues of roughly $300 billion in the next few years to break even on their 
average annual capex investments in 2026 and 2027. These estimates reflect their 
expectations for average annual AI capex in 2026 and 2027 for the US hyperscalers, 
including AMZN, GOOGL, META, MSFT, ORCL, and SPCX. The breakeven revenues 
required for this capex represent the sum of annual depreciation and operating 
expenses. Exhibit 8 provides an estimated sensitivity of required AI revenues the 
hyperscalers must generate in different return on invested capital (“ROIC”) scenarios. 
These required revenue estimates are anchored on average annual capex in 2026 and 
2027 and would argue for even larger estimates based on cumulative capex.

lines represent equal-weighted averages of growth implied by Anera and OpenRouter data

### Page 6

The hyperscalers’ current estimated AI revenues remain below the amount that 
would allow them to break even on their capex, but those revenues are growing 
quickly and revenue backlogs are sizable. The hyperscalers have started to monetize 
their AI investments. Year/year cloud revenue growth for AMZN, GOOGL, MSFT, and 
ORCL has accelerated from 25% in 2024 to 48% in Q2 2026. In dollar terms, annualized 
cloud revenues in excess of their 2024 growth rate registered nearly $70 billion in Q2 
and have been roughly doubling during the past few quarters. The three major US public 
cloud companies (AMZN, GOOGL, MSFT) have also announced a combined revenue 
backlog of $1.7 trillion, which provides line of sight into these companies’ ability to meet 
required revenue targets.

### Page 7

The magnitude of capex in 2026 and 2027 points to even larger “required” annual 
revenues in the next few years for the “application layer” of companies buying 
much of that compute from the hyperscalers. These revenues reflect the additional 
costs and profit margins of application companies on top of the “required” hyperscaler 
revenues selling compute. EBIT margins for large-cap software companies have 
averaged 40% during the past five years. We utilize EBIT margins rather than gross 
margins to include the costs of both training and inference, and assume additional 
expenses alongside compute. The range of potential required revenues is wide 
depending on which companies constitute the application layer and how their costs are 
structured.  
Exhibit 11 provides a range of potential “required” revenues based on hyperscaler 
ROIC and application layer EBIT margins. For example, the application layer would 
have to generate over $1 trillion in annual revenues if those companies had a 30% EBIT 
margin and the hyperscalers generated a 10%-20% return on invested capital. 
The “required” application layer revenues that would justify current capex are large 
but potentially achievable. For example, estimates of global advertising and software 
spending each equal roughly $1.5 trillion in 2026. Our economists estimate that total 
annual AI US labor productivity gains that will potentially accrue to capital total roughly 
$1.8 trillion. However, not all of these gains will necessarily accrue to the infrastructure 
and application layer companies. In addition, another year of strong capex growth in 
2028 would raise the hurdle further. AI-related revenues and capex needs would be 
substantially larger accounting for physical AI or the potential emergence of AGI.

growth rate

### Page 8

3. What outlook for AI is the equity market pricing? 
While market participants express skepticism about the durability of AI 
infrastructure stocks’ earnings growth, that view is at least partially priced. The 
median AI infrastructure stock has de-rated from 32x consensus forward earnings in 
April 2026 to 22x today. In addition to tracking standard P/E multiples, comparing 
recent changes in market cap to recent changes in earnings can signal how long the 
market expects current earnings strength to persist. Among various AI infrastructure 
baskets, that ratio remains most elevated among optical networking, electrical grid, and 
liquid cooling stocks while expectations appear most conservative among 
semiconductors and utilities. 
Semiconductor valuations appear to embed particularly skeptical views regarding 
the sustainability of current earnings strength. Valuations have declined sharply 
among Semis stocks, which is typical among cyclical companies with strong near-term 
growth but uncertain medium-term growth. We believe the investment opportunity is 
more compelling in fabless chip stocks than in memory stocks. Our equity analysts are 
Buy-rated on NVDA and AVGO but Neutral-rated on MU.

### Page 9

Memory stock prices imply earnings will decline in coming years, but the degree of 
pessimism does not appear particularly extreme relative to past cycles. Memory 
stocks currently trade at a 2-year forward P/E of 4x, half their 15-year average, but 
Memory firms currently enjoy gross margins of roughly 80%, more than double their 
historical average. To bring the current P/E of memory stocks to its 15-year average, 
earnings in coming years would need to decline by roughly 50%. While this decline is 
large in absolute terms, memory is a cyclical industry and has experienced larger EPS 
declines, including outright negative earnings, during previous cycles. This pricing will 
prove too pessimistic if 2028 forecasts are realized, but many investors have expressed 
skepticism about rotating back into higher beta pockets of the infrastructure trade as 
capex growth begins to normalize.

### Page 10

In contrast, the fabless chip stocks also trade at discounted valuations but have had 
less extreme margin expansion. Margins for the largest fabless chip stocks are only 
modestly above their historical average. Earnings in the next few years would need to 
decline by about 10% to bring the P/E back to its historical average. This is similar to the 
magnitude of historical realized cyclical earnings declines for the group.

The likelihood of decelerating capex growth should be a positive catalyst for the 
hyperscalers. We previously laid out three conditions for the hyperscalers to regain 
leadership. These include decelerating macroeconomic growth, demonstrated 
monetization of AI investment, and decelerating capex growth. Our economists believe 
US economic growth is no longer accelerating. The hyperscalers have already started to 
demonstrate signs of monetization, and we expect capex growth will slow in 2027. 
Uncertainty about the length, magnitude, and return on investment for this capex cycle 
will likely persist. However, the hyperscalers have de-rated to their lowest P/E multiples 
in over a decade, and the group now trades just four turns above the median S&P 500 
company, its smallest premium on record. While investors have recently been focused 
more on the hyperscalers’ negative free cash flows than on their earnings, visibility into 
improving FCF as revenues accelerate and capex decelerates should be supportive for 
the stocks.

### Page 13

Enterprise AI adoption remains in early stages but spending has recently 
accelerated, suggesting the impact of AI on corporate earnings should grow 
increasingly visible in coming quarters. The Census Bureau survey suggests that 40% 
of large US firms are using AI in the production of goods and services. Our analysis of Q2 
earnings commentary suggests roughly 50% of S&P 500 companies are using AI in the 
context of productivity, but very few companies have quantified the productivity uplift 
today. However, the revenue run rates of model providers and alternative measures of 
company spending on AI have accelerated during the last several months alongside the 
increasing use of agentic harnesses. 
In contrast with past periods of market exuberance, the equity market today does 
not appear to be pricing expectations of widespread productivity gains. Baskets of 
AI productivity beneficiaries have traded roughly sideways on a relative basis YTD, 
matching the trajectory of near-term forward EPS estimates. Similarly, the measure of 
market-implied long-term growth expectations in Exhibit 22 is only slightly above the 
historical average (for details, see US Equity Views: The next phases of the AI trade).

## Document 441: We Promised You Buying Opportunities After The Midterms

Source PDF: all report pdf/We Promised You Buying Opportunities After The Midterms.pdf
Report source: researchmail轉寄
Date: 2026-11-06
Pages: 7
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

From:    Yardeni QuickTakes <yardeni-research@ghost.io>
Subject: We Promised You Buying Opportunities After The Midterms
Date:    6 hours ago
We Promised You Buying Opportunities After The Midterms

YARDENI QUICKTAKES
We Promised You Buying
Opportunities After The
Midterms
By Ed Yardeni & Elias Griepentrog • 5 Nov 2026
View in browser
Photo by Artem Beliaikin / Unsplash

I. The Delayed Cold Shower
Since September 15, we've counseled proceeding with caution in the
stock market through the midterm elections. We anticipated buying
opportunities after Tuesday's elections. Stocks rallied on Wednesday. But
they plunged today.

### Page 2

The S&P 500 fell 4.4%, its worst day since the April 2025 tariff shock
(charts). The Nasdaq composite sank 5.5%, entering correction territory,
while the Dow dropped 3.4% and the PHLX Semiconductor index plunged
12.0%. The cold shower we warned might follow a Democratic sweep
arrived a day late.
We believe that Wednesday's rally was driven by short covering as two
key risks failed to materialize. First, investors feared President Donald
Trump might contest close elections, potentially triggering a constitutional

crisis. Instead, on Wednesday morning, he acknowledged the Democrats'
decisive victory and said he is willing to work with them. Second, investors
feared an immediate re-escalation of the war in Iran after the midterms.
But oil prices fell sharply on Wednesday on reports that the US and Iran
are pursuing a diplomatic solution.

### Page 3

On Thursday, investors began discounting the implications of the
Democratic sweep for the economy's golden goose: the AI investment
boom.
The political backlash against data centers is gaining momentum, with
76% of voters opposing new facilities in their communities, according to
NBC exit polls. Perhaps most consequentially, Texas, which accounts for
nearly 30% of planned US data center additions in 2028, elected Gina
Hinojosa as governor after she promised to halt new approvals. Goldman

Sachs estimates that states facing possible restrictions account for
roughly 45% of planned US data center capacity. Tougher permitting and
higher costs could delay billions in investment and postpone returns for
hyperscalers. Given the AI boom's growing importance to economic
activity, the implications extend well beyond technology.
As a result, the 10-year Treasury yield fell another 7bps to 4.83% today,
reflecting safe-haven demand and fears of weaker economic growth.

Investors also pared back Fed tightening expectations, with futures pricing
just 1.7 quarter-point hikes over the next 12 months (chart).
We view the political backlash as another constraint on AI investment,
alongside financial and physical bottlenecks. But these are speed bumps,
not roadblocks, even though Wall Street seems to be pricing in the latter.
We think that's overdone. As AI adoption broadens and productivity gains
accelerate, demand for compute will keep growing. Data centers will get

built, albeit at a slower pace. Speed bumps should prevent the AI boom
from moving too fast and crashing around the next curve.

### Page 4

That's why we view Thursday's selloff as a buying opportunity. Our favorite
Boom-Bust Ratios likely turned even more bearish after today's selloff
(chart). From a contrarian perspective, panic attacks tend to be bullish.
Our Roaring 2020s scenario remains on track, and we are sticking with
our 7,900 year-end S&P 500 target.
Late-breaking news: Texas Governor-elect Gina Hinojosa was interviewed
after the market closed on CNN and said, "All currently permitted data

centers in Texas will be allowed to finish construction." This essentially
covers all of 2027 and likely most of 2028. She plans to pursue legislation
requiring newly permitted data centers to pay taxes, protect consumers
from higher power prices, preserve water availability, use union labor, and
give "Texans a seat at the table."
II. On Productivity and Labor Market Conditions
While Wall Street frets about a potential slowdown in the AI investment

boom, the latest data show that productivity growth is already
accelerating, even as AI adoption remains in its early stages. Here is
more:
(1) Productivity. Nonfarm business productivity jumped 3.6% q/q (saar)
during Q3, the strongest pace since Q3-2025, while rising 2.1% y/y
(chart).

### Page 5

The seven-year productivity growth cycle is increasing by 2.4% annually,
exceeding its historical average (chart). In our Roaring 2020s scenario,
productivity growth is likely to rise to 3.5%-4.0% over the remainder of this
decade.
(2) Unit labor costs & inflation. Unit labor costs is measured as hourly
compensation divided by productivity. It rose just 2.4% y/y in Q3-2026,
reinforcing our view that the labor market is not currently a source of
inflationary pressure (chart).

### Page 6

(3) Unemployment claims. Initial jobless claims fell to just 189,000
during the week ended October 31 (chart). The four-week moving average
dropped to 194,750, its lowest level since 1969! Continuing claims also
remain low. The message is clear: The Fed has little reason to worry
about the health of the US labor market.
(4) Layoff announcements. Challenger reported just 44,000 announced
job cuts in October, down 71% y/y and the lowest October reading since

2023, reinforcing the message that layoffs remain subdued (chart).

### Page 7

clause.
